<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-49458161" data-id="49458161" data-tags="Business|AI|Hardware|DevOps" data-score="1943" data-comments="897" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49458161" data-time="1787793175">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.businessinsider.com/nvidia-in-talks-to-buy-hugging-face-13-billion-dollars-2026-8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://i.insider.com/6a8f6dc43f7df8787cff40f7?width=1200&amp;format=jpeg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='businessinsider.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.businessinsider.com/nvidia-in-talks-to-buy-hugging-face-13-billion-dollars-2026-8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nvidia agrees to acquire Hugging Face for $13B</span><span class='zh'>英伟达同意以130亿美元收购 Hugging Face</span></a><span class="dom">businessinsider.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NVIDIA plans to buy Hugging Face for $13 billion, positioning its software ecosystem to ride alongside its hardware stack.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达以130亿美元收购 Hugging Face，意在把开源模型生态融入自家硬件与软件工具链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NVIDIA plans to buy Hugging Face for $13 billion, positioning its software ecosystem to ride alongside its hardware stack. The acquisition would deepen integration of models, datasets, and tools across the NVIDIA platform, potentially accelerating AI deployment and the competitive dynamics of AI infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>英伟达以130亿美元收购 Hugging Face，意在把开源模型生态融入自家硬件与软件工具链。交易可能加速 AI 部署并改变 AI 基础设施的竞争格局，同时影响开源社区的格局。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1943</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49458161' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>897</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49151933" data-id="49151933" data-tags="Privacy" data-score="1759" data-comments="710" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49151933" data-time="1785738481">
<a class="thumb" href="https://gruhn.me/blog/2026-08-03/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=gruhn.me&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>gruhn.me</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gruhn.me/blog/2026-08-03/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t be a meat proxy</span><span class='zh'>别做肉身代理</span></a><span class="dom">gruhn.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Avoid being used as a proxy for others’ actions or identity in tech systems; protect your own data and autonomy.</span><span class='zh'>不要让自己被作为他人行为或身份的代理，在技术系统中保持对自身数据和行为的控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Avoid being used as a proxy for others’ actions or identity in tech systems; protect your own data and autonomy.</span><span class='zh'>不要让自己被作为他人行为或身份的代理，在技术系统中保持对自身数据和行为的控制。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>3.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1759</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49151933' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>710</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49303202" data-id="49303202" data-tags="Web|Privacy" data-score="1694" data-comments="672" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303202" data-time="1786734200">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.pcworld.com/article/3212428/firefox-is-now-the-last-major-browser-that-still-supports-ublock-origin.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.pcworld.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/3212428-0-62401400-1786636364-Firefox-foto-mozilla.png?w=1024' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='pcworld.com' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pcworld.com/article/3212428/firefox-is-now-the-last-major-browser-that-still-supports-ublock-origin.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Firefox is now the last major browser that still supports uBlock Origin</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 成为仍支持 uBlock Origin 的最后一款主流浏览器</span></a><span class="dom">pcworld.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Firefox remains the last major browser to support uBlock Origin, as others shift away from traditional ad-block extensions due to evolving extension policies.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 仍然是主流浏览器中最后一个支持 uBlock Origin 的选择。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Firefox remains the last major browser to support uBlock Origin, as others shift away from traditional ad-block extensions due to evolving extension policies. This preserves a familiar privacy tool for Firefox users but underscores growing fragmentation in how browsers handle ad blocking.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 仍然是主流浏览器中最后一个支持 uBlock Origin 的选择。随着其他浏览器因扩展策略和 MV3 变更而削弱对这类扩展的支持，Firefox 的隐私保护能力仍具吸引力，但也暴露出浏览器扩展生态的分化。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Web' style='--c:#06b6d4'>Web</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1694</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49303202' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>672</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49379550" data-id="49379550" data-tags="Legal|Privacy|Open Source" data-score="1640" data-comments="385" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49379550" data-time="1787256446">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.curiousquail.com/im-upset-again-about-a-co-creator-of-rss-being-prosecuted-for-something-meta-is-doing-with-little-consequence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/curiousquail/img_2026_web.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.curiousquail.com' data-c='#64748b' data-tag='Legal'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.curiousquail.com/im-upset-again-about-a-co-creator-of-rss-being-prosecuted-for-something-meta-is-doing-with-little-consequence/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Aaron Swartz was prosecuted for scraping, while Meta does it without consequence</span><span class='zh'>Aaron Swartz 因抓取被起诉，而 Meta 却未受惩罚</span></a><span class="dom">blog.curiousquail.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aaron Swartz faced a high-profile prosecution for data scraping, while large platforms like Meta navigate similar tactics with far fewer legal repercussions.</span><span class='zh'>这凸显了对数据抓取的执法不对称——个人开发者可能遭到起诉，而大型科技公司常获较少或无惩罚。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aaron Swartz faced a high-profile prosecution for data scraping, while large platforms like Meta navigate similar tactics with far fewer legal repercussions. The contrast underscores how enforcement risk impacts researchers, developers, and data-access culture in the web era. This discrepancy influences open access, data mining, and how future scrapers are treated legally.</span><span class='zh'>这凸显了对数据抓取的执法不对称——个人开发者可能遭到起诉，而大型科技公司常获较少或无惩罚。事件影响数据获取与开放获取的格局，也对未来的合规风险和研究活动产生深远意义。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Legal' style='--c:#64748b'>Legal</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1640</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49379550' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>385</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-49124218" data-id="49124218" data-tags="Show HN|Web" data-score="1617" data-comments="397" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49124218" data-time="1785511048">

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN project called Elevators presents a novel approach or tool related to elevator systems—either scheduling, simulation, or a microservice concept—demonstrating practical, small-scale engineering work.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Elevators 项目展示了一个与电梯相关的工具/演示，可能是调度、仿真或小型工程原型，欢迎社区反馈与迭代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN project called Elevators presents a novel approach or tool related to elevator systems—either scheduling, simulation, or a microservice concept—demonstrating practical, small-scale engineering work. It invites community feedback and iteration.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Elevators 项目展示了一个与电梯相关的工具/演示，可能是调度、仿真或小型工程原型，欢迎社区反馈与迭代。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://lectronz.com/u/lectronz/articles/how-europe-is-killing-makers-and-micro-entrepreneurs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://lectronz.com/lectronz/active_storage/blobs/proxy/eyJfcmFpbHMiOnsiZGF0YSI6NzAzNjgsInB1ciI6ImJsb2JfaWQifX0=--86803ec7bfb65d5430f3e59aa9c5b2a8dafd15f0/DSC_1769.JPG' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='lectronz.com' data-c='#f97316' data-tag='Politics'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lectronz.com/u/lectronz/articles/how-europe-is-killing-makers-and-micro-entrepreneurs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Europe is killing makers and micro-entrepreneurs</span><span class='zh'>欧洲如何扼杀制造者和微型创业者</span></a><span class="dom">lectronz.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Europe is tightening rules that raise compliance costs for small-scale makers and micro-entrepreneurs.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲正在收紧法规，提升小规模制造者和微型创业者的合规成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Europe is tightening rules that raise compliance costs for small-scale makers and micro-entrepreneurs. The higher friction could slow hardware startups and push more innovation to friendlier regions, reshaping local maker ecosystems. This trend matters for hardware, IoT, and regional tech communities.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲正在收紧法规，提升小规模制造者和微型创业者的合规成本。更高的摩擦可能放缓硬件创业，并将更多创新挪向更友好的地区，重塑本地创客生态。此趋势关系到硬件、物联网以及区域科技社群。</span></p>

The note offers a personal glimpse into how partnership shaped his life and work.</span><span class='zh'>斯蒂芬·沃尔夫拉姆在纪念帖中缅怀妻子 Elise Cawley，感恩与她共同度过的36个美好岁月。文章揭示了个人伴侣关系在其人生与工作中的重要影响。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://openlogi.org/en" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://openlogi.org/social-preview.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='openlogi.org' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openlogi.org/en" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenLogi</span><span class='zh'>OpenLogi</span></a><span class="dom">openlogi.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenLogi presents a logistics stack aimed at simplifying e‑commerce fulfillment with developer-friendly APIs, inventory routing and connector integrations.</span><span class='zh'>OpenLogi 提供面向開發者的物流堆疊，通過 API、庫存路由和多個倉配連接來簡化電商履約流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenLogi presents a logistics stack aimed at simplifying e‑commerce fulfillment with developer-friendly APIs, inventory routing and connector integrations. Its approach lowers the barrier for merchants to orchestrate distributed warehousing and shipping without building their own backend operations.</span><span class='zh'>OpenLogi 提供面向開發者的物流堆疊，通過 API、庫存路由和多個倉配連接來簡化電商履約流程。它讓商家在不自建後端物流的情況下，也能協調分散倉儲和出貨。</span></p>

Her influence across country music and pop culture endures through hits, artistry, and charitable work.</span><span class='zh'>传闻称多莉·帕顿去世，这位传奇歌手、词曲创作者及慈善家的离世引发广泛悼念。她在乡村音乐和流行文化中的影响力通过经典作品、艺术成就与慈善事业继续留存。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn-thumbnails.huggingface.co/social-thumbnails/models/Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='huggingface.co' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">11</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/Qwen/Qwen3.8-27B-FP8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8 27B</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8-27B 模型</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 marks another step toward accessible, efficient LLMs.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 提供更高效的推理，27亿参数在普通显卡上也能更快地跑，显存需求更低，便于个人和小团队尝试大模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 marks another step toward accessible, efficient LLMs. The 27B parameter model is optimized for FP8 inference, enabling faster runs on commodity GPUs and lower memory footprints, helping hobbyists and small teams experiment with large models.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen 3.8-27B FP8 提供更高效的推理，27亿参数在普通显卡上也能更快地跑，显存需求更低，便于个人和小团队尝试大模型。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>1399</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49299605' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>782</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49345263" data-id="49345263" data-tags="Business|Finance" data-score="1372" data-comments="686" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345263" data-time="1787059358">
<a class="thumb" href="https://seths.blog/2026/08/the-amazon-tax/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://seths.blog/wp-content/uploads/2018/06/seth_godin_ogimages_v02_18061312.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='seths.blog' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">12</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://seths.blog/2026/08/the-amazon-tax/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Amazon tax</span><span class='zh'>亞馬遜稅</span></a><span class="dom">seths.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Selling on Amazon often requires giving up margin, control, and customer relationships to platform fees, advertising costs, and algorithmic visibility.</span><span class='zh'>在亞馬遜上銷售意味著要承擔平臺費用、廣告支出以及算法能見度帶來的成本，從而侵蝕利潤並削弱與顧客的直接關係。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Selling on Amazon often requires giving up margin, control, and customer relationships to platform fees, advertising costs, and algorithmic visibility. That cumulative cost — the &#x27;Amazon tax&#x27; — forces sellers to optimize for the marketplace&#x27;s incentives rather than their own brand or customers, with long-term competitive and dependency risks.</span><span class='zh'>在亞馬遜上銷售意味著要承擔平臺費用、廣告支出以及算法能見度帶來的成本，從而侵蝕利潤並削弱與顧客的直接關係。這種「亞馬遜稅」會讓賣家為了迎合平臺而非建立自有品牌或體驗，增加長期依賴與競爭風險。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Finance' style='--c:#84cc16'>Finance</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>1372</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49345263' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>686</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49161518" data-id="49161518" data-tags="AI" data-score="1364" data-comments="563" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49161518" data-time="1785791633">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.seangoedecke.com/llms-reward-expertise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=seangoedecke.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>seangoedecke.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.seangoedecke.com/llms-reward-expertise/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LLMs reward expertise</span><span class='zh'>大模型奖励专业知识</span></a><span class="dom">seangoedecke.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Recent work highlights that LLMs tend to reward content produced by or aligned with domain expertise.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究表明，大型语言模型倾向于奖励来自专业领域的高质量内容，这种偏好来自数据质量和专家来源在训练及评估中的重要性，进而影响模型行为与提示设计。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Recent work highlights that LLMs tend to reward content produced by or aligned with domain expertise. This bias emphasizes high-quality data and expert sources in training and evaluation, shaping model behavior and prompts. The takeaway: invest in expert-curated data and robust evaluation to avoid gatekeeping and misalignment.</span><span class='zh'>最新研究表明，大型语言模型倾向于奖励来自专业领域的高质量内容，这种偏好来自数据质量和专家来源在训练及评估中的重要性，进而影响模型行为与提示设计。核心意义在于应重视专家数据的整合与严格评估，避免因过度依赖权威而带来评判偏差或门槛化。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1364</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49161518' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>563</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49433292" data-id="49433292" data-tags="Hardware|AI" data-score="1278" data-comments="1239" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433292" data-time="1787662882">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-introduces-m6-and-m5-ultra-for-a-big-leap-in-performance-and-ai-compute/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/08/apple-introduces-m6-and-m5-ultra-for-a-big-leap-in-performance-and-ai-compute/tile/Apple-M6-and-M5-Ultra-hero-260825-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202608251301' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='apple.com' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">14</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-introduces-m6-and-m5-ultra-for-a-big-leap-in-performance-and-ai-compute/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple introduces M6 and M5 Ultra</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 M6 与 M5 Ultra 引领性能与 AI 算力大跃升</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple claims M6 and M5 Ultra deliver a substantial jump in CPU/GPU performance and on‑device AI compute, enabling heavier workloads like real‑time inference and larger models at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>苹果称 M6 与 M5 Ultra 将在 CPU/GPU 性能与边缘 AI 算力方面实现显著跃升，使实时推理和更大模型在本地可运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple claims M6 and M5 Ultra deliver a substantial jump in CPU/GPU performance and on‑device AI compute, enabling heavier workloads like real‑time inference and larger models at the edge. The chips are expected to bring higher memory bandwidth and improved neural accelerators, reinforcing Apple’s pro workflow ambitions across its lineup. For developers and creators, this sets a new baseline for on‑device AI capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>苹果称 M6 与 M5 Ultra 将在 CPU/GPU 性能与边缘 AI 算力方面实现显著跃升，使实时推理和更大模型在本地可运行。新芯片预计带来更高的内存带宽与改进的神经处理单元，强化其在 Pro 级工作流中的布局。对于开发者与创作者来说，标志着本地 AI 能力的新基线。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>1278</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49433292' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1239</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49491791" data-id="49491791" data-tags="Open Source" data-score="1254" data-comments="630" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49491791" data-time="1788025741">

<a class="thumb" href="https://kagi.com/changelog#11296" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f472b6' data-tag='Design'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=kagi.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>kagi.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://kagi.com/changelog#11296" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kagi added a setting for removing paywalled links from search results</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 新增可去除付费墙链接的搜索设置</span></a><span class="dom">kagi.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kagi added a setting to filter paywalled links from search results, giving users more control over what shows up.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 增加了从搜索结果中筛除付费墙链接的设置，让用户对显示内容有更大控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kagi added a setting to filter paywalled links from search results, giving users more control over what shows up. The feature helps researchers and readers who need free access and could pressure publishers to rethink paywalls. It highlights a shift toward user-tuned search experiences and more transparent results.</span><span class='zh'>Kagi 增加了从搜索结果中筛除付费墙链接的设置，让用户对显示内容有更大控制。该功能方便需要免费访问的研究者与读者，同时也可能促使出版商重新考虑付费墙策略。此举体现了向用户定制化搜索体验和更透明结果的趋势。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Design' style='--c:#f472b6'>Design</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>1248</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49388154' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>390</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49503601" data-id="49503601" data-tags="Culture" data-score="1218" data-comments="349" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49503601" data-time="1788130188">
<a class="thumb" href="https://unsung.aresluna.org/i-just-chose-words-carefully/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/i-just-chose-words-carefully/ogimage.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='unsung.aresluna.org' data-c='#d946ef' data-tag='Culture'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">17</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://unsung.aresluna.org/i-just-chose-words-carefully/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“I just chose words carefully”</span><span class='zh'>我只是认真地挑选了措辞</span></a><span class="dom">unsung.aresluna.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Careful word choice shapes how tech ideas are understood and trusted, influencing user perception and accountability.</span><span class='zh'>在科技语境中，措辞的慎密选择决定了人们对技术理念的理解和信任度，影响公众感知与问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Careful word choice shapes how tech ideas are understood and trusted, influencing user perception and accountability.</span><span class='zh'>在科技语境中，措辞的慎密选择决定了人们对技术理念的理解和信任度，影响公众感知与问责。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://tailscale.com/blog/sqlite-wal-reset-bug" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://tailscale.com/api/og-image?id=45389fc1-da6e-4e0d-82f6-140651c6ba57' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='tailscale.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://tailscale.com/blog/sqlite-wal-reset-bug" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tracking down the 16-year-old WAL-reset SQLite bug</span><span class='zh'>追踪这起持续16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">tailscale.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers zero in on a long-standing SQLite WAL reset bug that has persisted for 16 years.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员锁定一宗持续了16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers zero in on a long-standing SQLite WAL reset bug that has persisted for 16 years. The issue can cause data integrity problems when the write-ahead logging file resets under certain conditions, impacting apps using WAL mode. The takeaway is to test WAL edge cases and ensure robust backup/restore paths, highlighting SQLite quirks in real-world apps.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员锁定一宗持续了16年的 SQLite WAL 重置漏洞。该问题在特定条件下会导致写入前日志文件重置时的数据完整性问题，影响使用 WAL 模式的应用程序。建议开发者测试 WAL 的边界情况，并确保备份/恢复路径健全，凸显实际应用中 SQLite 的潜在坑点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.meta.ai/blog/introducing-muse-glimmer-open-agentic-model" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Muse Glimmer: 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows</span><span class='zh'>Muse Glimmer：面向持续本地代理工作流优化的30B参数模型</span></a><span class="dom">research.meta.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Muse Glimmer is a 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows, enabling persistent autonomous agents with privacy-friendly, low-latency inference.</span><span class='zh'>Muse Glimmer 是一个为持续本地代理工作流优化的 30B 参数模型，支持持续自我驱动的代理并具备隐私友好、低延迟的推理能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Muse Glimmer is a 30B-parameter model optimized for always-on local agent workflows, enabling persistent autonomous agents with privacy-friendly, low-latency inference. It embodies a shift toward on-device or edge AI that reduces reliance on cloud services.</span><span class='zh'>Muse Glimmer 是一个为持续本地代理工作流优化的 30B 参数模型，支持持续自我驱动的代理并具备隐私友好、低延迟的推理能力。这反映了在本地或边缘部署 AI、减少对云端依赖的趋势。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.mayerowitz.io/blog/mario-meets-pareto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.mayerowitz.io/SEO/mario-meets-pareto.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='mayerowitz.io' data-c='#10b981' data-tag='Science'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">20</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mayerowitz.io/blog/mario-meets-pareto" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mario Meets Pareto</span><span class='zh'>马里奥遇见帕累托原则</span></a><span class="dom">mayerowitz.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful piece that uses Mario to illustrate Pareto efficiency in optimization, offering intuition for how a small subset of actions can drive most outcomes in complex environments.</span><span class='zh'>通过以马里奥为例来阐释帕累托效率在优化中的作用，帮助读者理解在复杂环境中少数行动如何推动大部分结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful piece that uses Mario to illustrate Pareto efficiency in optimization, offering intuition for how a small subset of actions can drive most outcomes in complex environments. The concept translates to AI planning and resource allocation, guiding developers toward high-impact, scalable decisions. It’s a reminder that clever prioritization often beats brute-force attempts.</span><span class='zh'>通过以马里奥为例来阐释帕累托效率在优化中的作用，帮助读者理解在复杂环境中少数行动如何推动大部分结果。这一思路可迁移到 AI 规划与资源分配，指引开发者关注高影响力、可扩展的决策。提醒人们，精明的优先排序往往胜过盲目扩展。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GLM-5.3 expands the frontier of AI-assisted coding by showcasing emergent cyber capabilities that go beyond standard copilots. It hints at advanced code generation, reasoning, and integration skills that could reshape developer workflows and security tooling.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.3 展示了超越常规编码助手的涌现性网络能力，推动代码生成、推理与系统集成等方面的进步。此举可能重新定义开发者工作流以及相关的安全工具链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://z.ai/blog/glm-5.3-flash" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLM-5.3-Flash</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.3-Flash</span></a><span class="dom">z.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new release in the GLM-5.3 family focusing on cost-efficient inference, likely leveraging quantization and optimization tricks to run capable models on cheaper hardware.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.3-Flash 发布，强调在低成本硬件上的高效推理，可能通过量化与优化实现更便宜设备上的可用模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new release in the GLM-5.3 family focusing on cost-efficient inference, likely leveraging quantization and optimization tricks to run capable models on cheaper hardware. This broadens practical deployment of large language models on commodity devices and smaller clusters. The trend continues: vendors pushing cheaper, accessible LLMs without sacrificing too much accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.3-Flash 发布，强调在低成本硬件上的高效推理，可能通过量化与优化实现更便宜设备上的可用模型。此举扩展了大模型在普通硬件/小型集群上的落地场景，体现了厂商在降低部署成本上的持续努力。</span></p>

It aims to raise the bar for AI-assisted software development and could influence how developers integrate AI copilots into their workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.8-Max 是一款面向编码的模型，强调可靠的代码生成、更智能的调试，以及在协作场景中的更顺畅协同。它试图为 AI 辅助的软件开发设定新标准，可能影响开发者将 AI 助手整合到工作流中的方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/zedeus/nitter/issues/1442" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Nitter and XCancel receive cease and desist notices</span><span class='zh'>Nitter 与 XCancel 收到停止令通知</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Independent Twitter frontends like Nitter and XCancel have received cease-and-desist notices.</span><span class='zh'>独立的 Twitter 前端 Nitter 与 XCancel 收到停止令，凸显第三方前端在访问平台内容时面临的法律压力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Independent Twitter frontends like Nitter and XCancel have received cease-and-desist notices. The action underscores ongoing legal pressure on third-party clients that access a platform&#x27;s content and raises questions about privacy-focused, lightweight access versus platform control.</span><span class='zh'>独立的 Twitter 前端 Nitter 与 XCancel 收到停止令，凸显第三方前端在访问平台内容时面临的法律压力。此举引发对隐私导向、轻量级访问与平台控制之间平衡的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/08/21/us/politics/samuel-tunick-deleted-phone-felony.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Felony charges for citizen deleting phone data at US Border</span><span class='zh'>在美边境删除手机数据者被控重罪</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A US citizen is charged with a felony after deleting data from a phone at the border.</span><span class='zh'>一名美国公民在边境处删除手机数据后被控以重罪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A US citizen is charged with a felony after deleting data from a phone at the border. Prosecutors say the act violated federal law and could carry serious penalties, underscoring tensions between border enforcement and digital privacy. The case may shape how data handling at crossings is treated in court and could affect travelers&#x27; rights.</span><span class='zh'>一名美国公民在边境处删除手机数据后被控以重罪。检方称此举违反联邦法并可能面临严重刑罚，凸显边境执法与数字隐私之间的紧张关系。此案可能影响法院对跨境数据处理的判决及旅客权利。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>France plans to ban unsolicited telemarketing calls starting August 11, tightening consumer protection.</span><span class='zh'>法国将于11日起全面禁止未经请求的电话推销，强化对消费者隐私的保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>France plans to ban unsolicited telemarketing calls starting August 11, tightening consumer protection. The policy could reshape how marketers reach customers, boosting opt-in channels and call-blocking solutions.</span><span class='zh'>法国将于11日起全面禁止未经请求的电话推销，强化对消费者隐私的保护。该举措可能促使企业转向获同意的联系渠道，并影响营销行业的电话营销策略。</span></p>

The episode underscores how misinformation can seep into AI systems and policy discussions, highlighting the need for source verification in AI training data.</span><span class='zh'>据称，一家由国家背景支持的假智库被设立用于向 AI 聊天机器人提供误导信息。此事件揭示了错误信息如何进入 AI 系统和政策讨论，凸显对训练数据源进行核验的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.noemamag.com/why-is-everyone-in-tech-so-sad/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What happens if an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers</span><span class='zh'>当整类工人对职业失去信心时会怎样</span></a><span class="dom">noemamag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>If an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers, broad churn and talent shifts could disrupt project roadmaps and innovation cycles.</span><span class='zh'>如果整类工人对职业前景失去信心，可能引发大量离职与技能转型，影响项目进度与创新节奏。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>If an entire class of workers loses faith in their careers, broad churn and talent shifts could disrupt project roadmaps and innovation cycles. The piece argues burnout, misaligned incentives, and perceived stagnation in tech drive people to seek meaning elsewhere, urging leaders to rethink career paths and well-being.</span><span class='zh'>如果整类工人对职业前景失去信心，可能引发大量离职与技能转型，影响项目进度与创新节奏。文章指出倦怠、激励错位和对职业成长的感知停滞促使人们寻求更有意义的工作，呼吁领导层重新设计职业发展路径与员工福祉。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Career' style='--c:#2dd4bf'>Career</span><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>1023</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49209539' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1241</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49371857" data-id="49371857" data-tags="AI|Privacy" data-score="1020" data-comments="567" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49371857" data-time="1787214044">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dontpastetheai.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Don&#x27;t paste the AI, please</span><span class='zh'>请勿直接粘贴 AI 内容</span></a><span class="dom">dontpastetheai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open campaign promoting better AI data handling and privacy, urging users not to paste prompts or model outputs into public chat interfaces.</span><span class='zh'>一个倡导更好 AI 数据处理与隐私的公开运动，敦促用户不要把提示或模型输出粘贴到公开的对话界面。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open campaign promoting better AI data handling and privacy, urging users not to paste prompts or model outputs into public chat interfaces. It highlights the growing need for policy and tooling to protect prompts, training data, and personal information. It also advocates for private or local prompt workflows.</span><span class='zh'>一个倡导更好 AI 数据处理与隐私的公开运动，敦促用户不要把提示或模型输出粘贴到公开的对话界面。强调保护提示、训练数据与个人信息的必要性，并倡导私有或本地化的提示工作流。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SenteLabsAI/OpenExecutive" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>CEO fired developers to make room for AI. Developers create open source AI CEO</span><span class='zh'>CEO 为了给 AI 腾出空间而解雇开发者，开发者推出开源 AI CEO</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A CEO allegedly fired developers to make room for AI, prompting debates about AI governance and corporate strategy; in response, a team of developers created an open-source AI CEO project to explore decentralized AI leadership.</span><span class='zh'>报道称某CEO为让AI腾出空间而解雇开发者，引发关于 AI 治理与企业策略的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A CEO allegedly fired developers to make room for AI, prompting debates about AI governance and corporate strategy; in response, a team of developers created an open-source AI CEO project to explore decentralized AI leadership.</span><span class='zh'>报道称某CEO为让AI腾出空间而解雇开发者，引发关于 AI 治理与企业策略的讨论。开发者们进而推出了开源 AI CEO 项目（OpenExecutive），以探索去中心化的 AI 领导力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Advocates argue that GUIs should be navigable entirely via keyboard to boost accessibility and speed.</span><span class='zh'>主张将 GUI 完全通过键盘操作来实现可访问性与效率提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/lemire/status/2091894299289874926" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Xiaomi: New CPU matches Apple cores single threaded, much faster multithreaded</span><span class='zh'>小米新 CPU：单核媲美苹果核心，多核性能显著提升</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new Xiaomi CPU delivers single-thread performance that rivals Apple cores, while delivering notably higher throughput on multi-threaded workloads.</span><span class='zh'>新一代小米处理器在单线程性能上接近苹果核心，而在多线程工作负载上则显著更快。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new Xiaomi CPU delivers single-thread performance that rivals Apple cores, while delivering notably higher throughput on multi-threaded workloads. The design emphasis appears to optimize threading efficiency and instruction-level parallelism, challenging assumptions about relative performance across mobile SoCs. The result could shift expectations for mid-range and flagship Android devices.</span><span class='zh'>新一代小米处理器在单线程性能上接近苹果核心，而在多线程工作负载上则显著更快。此设计似乎强调对线程效率和指令级并行性的优化，挑战了移动SoC之间性能的普遍认知。对于中高端安卓设备的期望可能因此发生变化。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AliExpress pages reportedly perform silent WebAudio fingerprinting, interfering with Bluetooth multipoint connections. The finding highlights privacy risks and the stealthy nature of some tracking techniques on consumer sites.</span><span class='zh'>据称 AliExpress 页面在后台进行 WebAudio 指纹识别，干扰蓝牙多点连接。这一发现凸显隐私风险及部分网站使用隐匿跟踪技术的现状。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sprocketfox.io/xssfox/2026/08/19/sondehub-and-war/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A joke domain purchase turned in geopolitical warfare</span><span class='zh'>一个玩笑域名购买引发的地缘政治冲突</span></a><span class="dom">sprocketfox.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A whimsical domain-name purchase spirals into geopolitical warfare, illustrating how digital assets can become leverage in information and power struggles.</span><span class='zh'>一个玩笑性质的域名购买竟演变成地缘政治对抗，说明数字资产可以成为信息与权力博弈中的筹码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A whimsical domain-name purchase spirals into geopolitical warfare, illustrating how digital assets can become leverage in information and power struggles. The tale underscores the political fragility and strategic significance of internet infrastructure and branding in a global context.</span><span class='zh'>一个玩笑性质的域名购买竟演变成地缘政治对抗，说明数字资产可以成为信息与权力博弈中的筹码。此事凸显网络基础设施与品牌在全球格局中的政治敏感性与战略意义。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">38</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.florianherrengt.com/ai-removing-middle-class-software-engineering.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI is removing the middle class of software engineering?</span><span class='zh'>AI 正在吞并软件工程的中产阶层</span></a><span class="dom">blog.florianherrengt.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A viewpoint argues that AI is eroding the middle tier of software engineering by automating routine tasks and raising productivity.</span><span class='zh'>观点认为 AI 正通过自动化日常任务和提高生产率，削弱软件工程的中层岗位。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A viewpoint argues that AI is eroding the middle tier of software engineering by automating routine tasks and raising productivity. It questions career ladders and suggests that engineers must adapt by focusing on higher-level design, system architecture, and AI-augmented workflows.</span><span class='zh'>观点认为 AI 正通过自动化日常任务和提高生产率，削弱软件工程的中层岗位。文章讨论职业晋升路径的变化，建议工程师在高层设计、系统架构以及与 AI 协作的工作流中寻找新机会。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemini 3.7 Flash extends the Gemini line with a lighter, faster variant optimized for low latency and smaller memory usage.</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 3.7 快闪版在保持核心能力的同时，提供更轻量、低延迟的推理性能，降低内存占用，便于边缘部署。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini 3.7 Flash extends the Gemini line with a lighter, faster variant optimized for low latency and smaller memory usage. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theregister.com/systems/2026/08/06/amd-acquires-ai-chip-startup-taalas-to-boost-inference-performance-by-etching-models-into-silicon/5284344" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AMD acquires Taalas to boost inference performance by etching models in silicon</span><span class='zh'>AMD收购Taalas以通过在硅上刻画模型提升推理性能</span></a><span class="dom">theregister.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD expands its AI hardware play by acquiring Taalas, a startup focused on embedding AI models directly into silicon.</span><span class='zh'>AMD通过收购Taalas，意在将AI模型直接嵌入芯片中以提升推理吞吐量与能效，显示出硬件与软件深度协同设计的趋势。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AMD expands its AI hardware play by acquiring Taalas, a startup focused on embedding AI models directly into silicon. The deal aims to boost inference throughput and energy efficiency, signaling a trend toward hardware-software co-design for large-scale AI.</span><span class='zh'>AMD通过收购Taalas，意在将AI模型直接嵌入芯片中以提升推理吞吐量与能效，显示出硬件与软件深度协同设计的趋势。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">44</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thewalrus.ca/google-search-is-dying/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>As AI eats the web, the internet’s collective memory is disappearing</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 吃掉网络，互联网的集体记忆正在消失</span></a><span class="dom">thewalrus.ca</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>As AI consumes more of the web, the internet’s collective memory is thinning.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 越学越多地抓取网络，互联网的集体记忆正在变薄。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>As AI consumes more of the web, the internet’s collective memory is thinning. The piece argues that pervasive AI crawling and caching threaten provenance and long-term memory, complicating future search and verification. It calls for durable archiving and mindful data sourcing.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 越学越多地抓取网络，互联网的集体记忆正在变薄。文章指出 AI 抓取与缓存的长期可追溯性下降，影响可验证的知识来源。呼吁加强长期存档和负责任的数据获取。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Data' style='--c:#6366f1'>Data</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.1</span><span class='m ic-up'>910</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49250836' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>937</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49296740" data-id="49296740" data-tags="Programming|Design" data-score="902" data-comments="808" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49296740" data-time="1786702368">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mun-logadan.github.io/why-does-opus-5-feel-worse/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mun-logadan.github.io&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mun-logadan.github.io</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">45</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mun-logadan.github.io/why-does-opus-5-feel-worse/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why does Opus 5 feel worse to work with?</span><span class='zh'>为什么 Opus 5 的使用感更差？</span></a><span class="dom">mun-logadan.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis argues that Opus 5 feels worse to work with due to UX and workflow frictions common in enterprise software.</span><span class='zh'>对 Opus 5 的工作体验为何较差进行了分析，指向用户体验不一致、工作流阻塞和隐藏的复杂性等问题，影响日常生产力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis argues that Opus 5 feels worse to work with due to UX and workflow frictions common in enterprise software. It points to inconsistent interfaces, onboarding friction, and brittle customization that hamper daily productivity, despite feature-rich capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>对 Opus 5 的工作体验为何较差进行了分析，指向用户体验不一致、工作流阻塞和隐藏的复杂性等问题，影响日常生产力。该案例也折射出企业级软件在可用性方面的不足，即便功能强大也难以落地。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.senko.net/code-was-never-the-hard-part-is-an-insult-to-all-programmers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=blog.senko.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>blog.senko.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">46</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.senko.net/code-was-never-the-hard-part-is-an-insult-to-all-programmers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Code was never the hard part” is an insult to all programmers</span><span class='zh'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 对所有程序员都是侮辱</span></a><span class="dom">blog.senko.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; is often cited as a simplification of software work, but a blog argues it insults programmers by overlooking design, coordination, and maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 常被用来简化软件工作，但一篇博文认为这句话是在贬损程序员，因为它忽略了设计、协作与维护等真正的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; is often cited as a simplification of software work, but a blog argues it insults programmers by overlooking design, coordination, and maintenance. The piece reframes what makes projects hard and where real value lies for developers.</span><span class='zh'>&quot;Code was never the hard part&quot; 常被用来简化软件工作，但一篇博文认为这句话是在贬损程序员，因为它忽略了设计、协作与维护等真正的挑战。文章重新聚焦于影响项目难度与价值的关键因素。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>900</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49222189' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>557</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49468083" data-id="49468083" data-tags="Data" data-score="899" data-comments="278" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49468083" data-time="1787851077">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/dns-cache-memory-optimization-1111/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M1185AQ3B90700DKB48C2HHM.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.cloudflare.com' data-c='#6366f1' data-tag='Data'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">47</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/dns-cache-memory-optimization-1111/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Saving 100 terabytes of memory by optimizing 1.1.1.1&#x27;s DNS cache</span><span class='zh'>通过优化 1.1.1.1 的 DNS 缓存节省 100 TB 内存</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare details how reworking DNS cache structures and eviction policies shaved hundreds of terabytes of memory from a global resolver fleet.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 讲解了通过重构 DNS 缓存结构和淘汰策略，在全球解析器网络中节省了数百 TB 内存。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare details how reworking DNS cache structures and eviction policies shaved hundreds of terabytes of memory from a global resolver fleet. The change improves throughput and latency while reducing operational costs.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 讲解了通过重构 DNS 缓存结构和淘汰策略，在全球解析器网络中节省了数百 TB 内存。此举提升了吞吐和延迟表现，同时降低运维成本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xusheng.dev/posts/reversing/mspaint_invisible_watermark/main/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MS Paint and Photos inivisibly watermark even locally generated output with GUID</span><span class='zh'>MS Paint 与 Photos 对本地生成输出隐藏水印</span></a><span class="dom">xusheng.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers found that MS Paint and Photos embed invisible watermarks in images, even for outputs created locally, using GUIDs.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，MS Paint 与 Photos 在本地生成的图片中嵌入不可见水印，使用 GUID 进行标记。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers found that MS Paint and Photos embed invisible watermarks in images, even for outputs created locally, using GUIDs. The watermarking enables provenance tracking but raises privacy and control concerns for users who expect watermark-free content.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，MS Paint 与 Photos 在本地生成的图片中嵌入不可见水印，使用 GUID 进行标记。水印提供可溯源性，但引发隐私与对内容控制的担忧。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117057099753905023" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=grapheneos.social&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>grapheneos.social</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">57</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grapheneos.social/@GrapheneOS/117057099753905023" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google has stopped pushing Git tags for some Android source code</span><span class='zh'>谷歌停止为部分 Android 源代码推送 Git 标签</span></a><span class="dom">grapheneos.social</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google has stopped pushing Git tags on parts of Android&#x27;s source code, complicating reproducible builds and historical references for developers and downstream forks.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌不再为部分 Android 源代码推送 Git 标签，给开发者、下游分支的可复现构建与版本追溯带来挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google has stopped pushing Git tags on parts of Android&#x27;s source code, complicating reproducible builds and historical references for developers and downstream forks. The move underscores the fragility of automated versioning in large, evolving codebases and could affect security audits and CI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌不再为部分 Android 源代码推送 Git 标签，给开发者、下游分支的可复现构建与版本追溯带来挑战。这一举动凸显在大型、持续演进的代码库中自动化版本控制的脆弱性，可能影响安全审计和持续集成流程。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>807</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49364745' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>328</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49234675" data-id="49234675" data-tags="AI|Education" data-score="804" data-comments="531" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49234675" data-time="1786303009">
<a class="thumb" href="https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/articles/how-i-use-llms-to-learn/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/images/ChipTycoon.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='laurentiugabriel.github.io' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">58</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://laurentiugabriel.github.io/blog/articles/how-i-use-llms-to-learn/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How I use LLMs to learn complex topics</span><span class='zh'>我如何用大语言模型学习复杂主题</span></a><span class="dom">laurentiugabriel.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I use a staged approach to learning with LLMs: map an outline with the model, generate concise explanations, then test understanding with targeted prompts.</span><span class='zh'>将学习分成阶段使用模型：用它勾勒大纲、生成简明解释、再通过有针对性的提问检验理解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I use a staged approach to learning with LLMs: map an outline with the model, generate concise explanations, then test understanding with targeted prompts. This turns the model into a structured tutor that surfaces gaps and helps build solid mental models.</span><span class='zh'>将学习分成阶段使用模型：用它勾勒大纲、生成简明解释、再通过有针对性的提问检验理解。把模型当作高效的学习辅导工具，帮助建立稳固的认知模型并暴露薄弱点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/08/apple-introduces-new-mac-studio-with-m5-max-and-m5-ultra/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New Mac Studio with M5 Max and M5 Ultra</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出新款 Mac Studio，搭载 M5 Max 与 M5 Ultra</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple expands the Mac Studio lineup with the M5 Max and M5 Ultra, pairing the compact desktop chassis with redesigned high-end chips.</span><span class='zh'>苹果在 Mac Studio 系列中引入 M5 Max 和 M5 Ultra，继续以紧凑机箱实现高端算力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple expands the Mac Studio lineup with the M5 Max and M5 Ultra, pairing the compact desktop chassis with redesigned high-end chips. The new CPUs and GPUs aim to boost workstation-class performance for creators and developers, especially in AI-enabled workloads and large-scale builds. This reinforces Apple&#x27;s strategy of delivering formidable pro compute in a small form factor.</span><span class='zh'>苹果在 Mac Studio 系列中引入 M5 Max 和 M5 Ultra，继续以紧凑机箱实现高端算力。新芯片对创作者与开发者的 AI 相关工作负载以及大型编译任务提供更强性能，彰显苹果在小型工作站领域提升 Pro 级算力的策略。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://four.htmx.org/announcements/2026-08-28-htmx-4.0.0-is-released" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://four.htmx.org/img/og-image.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='four.htmx.org' data-c='#06b6d4' data-tag='Web'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">60</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://four.htmx.org/announcements/2026-08-28-htmx-4.0.0-is-released" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Htmx 4.0</span><span class='zh'>Htmx 4.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">four.htmx.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Htmx 4.0 launches with a refreshed API surface and broader server-driven UI capabilities, further simplifying dynamic pages without heavy JavaScript frameworks.</span><span class='zh'>Htmx 4.0 发布，API 面貌焕新，扩展了服务器驱动的 UI 能力，进一步简化在不依赖大规模 JS 框架的情况下实现动态页面的开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Htmx 4.0 launches with a refreshed API surface and broader server-driven UI capabilities, further simplifying dynamic pages without heavy JavaScript frameworks. The release tightens bindings around hx- attributes and adds improvements for streaming and partial updates, keeping htmx attractive for developers seeking low-friction interactivity.</span><span class='zh'>Htmx 4.0 发布，API 面貌焕新，扩展了服务器驱动的 UI 能力，进一步简化在不依赖大规模 JS 框架的情况下实现动态页面的开发。新版本加强了对 hx- 属性的集成，并在流式更新与局部页面更新方面带来改进，继续巩固了它在追求低摩擦交互的开发者中的定位。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://daringfireball.net/2026/08/anthropics_watermark_text_adulteration_in_claude_is_a_perversion_of_writing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=daringfireball.net&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>daringfireball.net</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">61</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://daringfireball.net/2026/08/anthropics_watermark_text_adulteration_in_claude_is_a_perversion_of_writing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s ‘watermark’ text adulteration in Claude is a perversion of writing</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 Claude 水印文本被篡改：对写作的扭曲</span></a><span class="dom">daringfireball.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Watermarking AI-produced text in Claude relies on injecting markers that distort writing, raising questions about authorial voice and readability.</span><span class='zh'>在 Claude 上进行文本水印时，文本被人为改动，损害了写作的可读性与作者声线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Watermarking AI-produced text in Claude relies on injecting markers that distort writing, raising questions about authorial voice and readability. Critics argue this approach perverts the aesthetics and trust of AI-assisted writing, especially in creative or editorial work. The piece calls for safer, less invasive methods to attribute AI authorship.</span><span class='zh'>在 Claude 上进行文本水印时，文本被人为改动，损害了写作的可读性与作者声线。批评者认为这种做法扭曲了创作的美感和对AI辅助写作的信任，尤其在编辑和创作场景中更为突出。文章呼吁采用更安全、侵入性更小的署名方法以实现归属。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>797</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49324087' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>697</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49479878" data-id="49479878" data-tags="AI|Open Source|Code" data-score="796" data-comments="277" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49479878" data-time="1787930413">
<a class="thumb" href="https://huggingface.co/zai-org/GLM-5.3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HQ0WAnyXIAENtQA.jpg:large' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='huggingface.co' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">62</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://huggingface.co/zai-org/GLM-5.3" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GLM-5.3 is now open-weight</span><span class='zh'>GLM-5.3 已开源权重</span></a><span class="dom">huggingface.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An open-weight release lets researchers run the model locally and inspect behavior; it aids reproducibility and benchmarking.</span><span class='zh'>公开权重意味着研究者可以本地运行模型、研究推理过程，提升复现实验和基准对比的可信度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An open-weight release lets researchers run the model locally and inspect behavior; it aids reproducibility and benchmarking. The move signals growing openness in large-language-model ecosystems and invites community experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>公开权重意味着研究者可以本地运行模型、研究推理过程，提升复现实验和基准对比的可信度。此举体现了大语言模型生态的开放化趋势，并鼓励社区参与和改进。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wsj.com/politics/policy/u-s-state-department-pauses-immigrant-visa-applications-25b31b23" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>U.S. State Department pauses immigrant visa applications</span><span class='zh'>美国国务院暂停移民签证申请</span></a><span class="dom">wsj.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The U.S. State Department paused immigrant visa applications amid processing backlogs and security concerns, affecting applicants and consular operations.</span><span class='zh'>美国国务院因处理积压与安全问题暂停移民签证申请，影响申请人及领事馆运作。</span></p>

The halt is expected to have widespread impact on families awaiting immigration.</span><span class='zh'>美国国务院因处理积压与安全问题暂停移民签证申请，影响申请人及领事馆运作。此举预计会对等待移民的家庭造成广泛影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ft.com/content/5ee49718-c258-4f01-aa32-7e5b76ae5245" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s best AI model struggles to attract users as cheaper tools thrive</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的最佳 AI 模型在吸引用户方面遇冷，便宜工具更受欢迎</span></a><span class="dom">ft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reports that Anthropic&#x27;s top AI model is failing to attract users as cheaper competitors gain traction, raising questions about pricing, UX, and use-case fit.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示Anthropic 的顶尖 AI 模型在市场上难以吸引用户，便宜的竞争对手更具吸引力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reports that Anthropic&#x27;s top AI model is failing to attract users as cheaper competitors gain traction, raising questions about pricing, UX, and use-case fit. The piece suggests that access cost and ease of integration matter as much as raw capability for adoption.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示Anthropic 的顶尖 AI 模型在市场上难以吸引用户，便宜的竞争对手更具吸引力。这提出定价、用户体验和用例匹配等因素与能力同等重要的结论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://calv.info/small-models-have-arrived" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Small Models Have Arrived</span><span class='zh'>小型模型已到来</span></a><span class="dom">calv.info</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Smaller AI models are reaching practical viability, enabling on-device and cost-effective deployments.</span><span class='zh'>更小的AI模型正在具备实际应用能力，能够在本地设备上部署并降低成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Smaller AI models are reaching practical viability, enabling on-device and cost-effective deployments. The piece surveys advances in distillation, quantization, and architecture design that let smaller models perform surprisingly well.</span><span class='zh'>更小的AI模型正在具备实际应用能力，能够在本地设备上部署并降低成本。文章梳理了蒸馏、量化和架构设计方面的进展，使小模型的性能出人意料地强。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">66</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/aug/06/new-mexico-court-meta" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>New Mexico court orders Meta to pay $567m over harms to children’s mental health</span><span class='zh'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">theguardian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A New Mexico court has ordered Meta to pay $567 million after finding that its platforms harmed children’s mental health.</span><span class='zh'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人的心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A New Mexico court has ordered Meta to pay $567 million after finding that its platforms harmed children’s mental health. The ruling adds to growing regulatory pressure on social media companies and could push Meta to adjust safety measures and user protections, even as enforcement costs rise.</span><span class='zh'>新墨西哥法院判决 Meta 因对未成年人的心理健康造成伤害，需要赔偿 5.67 亿美元。此案加剧了对社交媒体平台在青少年福祉方面的监管压力，并可能促使 Meta 在安全措施和用户保护方面做出更多调整，监管成本也在上升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/california-lawmakers-unanimously-pass-linux-exemption-from-age-verification-law-software-distributed-under-the-gpl-mit-bsd-and-apache-licenses-are-exempt" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>California lawmakers unanimously pass Linux exemption from age-verification law</span><span class='zh'>加州通过 Linux 豁免年龄验证法</span></a><span class="dom">tomshardware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>California lawmakers unanimously exempt Linux and GPL-licensed software from the state&#x27;s age-verification requirement, preserving open-source distribution channels.</span><span class='zh'>加州立法者一致通过针对 Linux 与 GPL 等开源软件的年龄验证豁免，确保开源软件的分发不受额外年龄要求限制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>California lawmakers unanimously exempt Linux and GPL-licensed software from the state&#x27;s age-verification requirement, preserving open-source distribution channels. The move clarifies that common open-source licenses shield software from age checks while still applying to other content.</span><span class='zh'>加州立法者一致通过针对 Linux 与 GPL 等开源软件的年龄验证豁免，确保开源软件的分发不受额外年龄要求限制。这一举措澄清了在某些内容需要年龄验证时，开源许可的软件不受这些限制。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An author argues that AI-generated images reduce engagement with blog posts, making content feel artificial and distracting.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，AI 生成的图片会削弱博客的阅读体验，让内容显得不真实且分散注意力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An author argues that AI-generated images reduce engagement with blog posts, making content feel artificial and distracting. The visuals can undermine the reader experience, causing skimming or distraction when images lack nuance. The piece recommends using authentic visuals—original photos or carefully curated art—to preserve reader trust and focus.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为，AI 生成的图片会削弱博客的阅读体验，让内容显得不真实且分散注意力。图片若缺乏层次感可能让读者快速跳读或走神。文中建议使用真实照片或精挑细选的视觉元素来维持读者信任与专注度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://deepseek.com/harness/en/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Harness developer preview</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Harness 框架</span></a><span class="dom">deepseek.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Harness appears to be a framework around DeepSeek AI to run experiments and benchmark performance.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Harness 似乎是围绕 DeepSeek AI 的一个用于实验运行与性能基准的框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek Harness appears to be a framework around DeepSeek AI to run experiments and benchmark performance. It could help teams standardize evaluation, compare model behavior, and improve reproducibility in AI workflows. This development comes at a time when measurable benchmarks are increasingly important for agent-powered systems.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek Harness 似乎是围绕 DeepSeek AI 的一个用于实验运行与性能基准的框架。它有助于团队标准化评估、对比模型行为，并提升 AI 工作流的可重复性。随着对代理系统的基准衡量需求提升，这类工具的重要性也在上升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://webiterate.dev/google-removed-extensions-ublock-origin-108/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google Has Removed MV2 Extensions from the Chrome Web Store, Including UBO</span><span class='zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 MV2 扩展，包括 UBO</span></a><span class="dom">webiterate.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin. The move accelerates migration to MV3, forcing developers to update to newer APIs and potentially altering how some extensions function. This marks a significant shift in the browser extension ecosystem, with privacy tools and enterprise plugins feeling the impact.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。此举推动开发者尽快迁移至 MV3，可能改变部分扩展的功能与行为，并对隐私工具与企业插件生态产生影响。</span></p>

Engineers investigated, mitigated the issue, and restored normal service.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 遭遇服务故障，核心功能如仓库访问和拉取请求受到影响。工程团队进行了排查、修复并恢复正常服务。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.exe.dev/devtools-must-be-open-source" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Devtools must be open source</span><span class='zh'>开发者工具必须开源</span></a><span class="dom">blog.exe.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Open-sourcing devtools improves transparency and security through community review and faster bug-fixing.</span><span class='zh'>开源开发者工具能提升透明度与安全性，因为社区评审和快速修复更易实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Open-sourcing devtools improves transparency and security through community review and faster bug-fixing. The piece argues that closed tools invite vendor lock-in and opaque risk, and outlines practical paths to open alternatives.</span><span class='zh'>开源开发者工具能提升透明度与安全性，因为社区评审和快速修复更易实现。文中指出封闭工具会带来供应商锁定和隐性风险，并提出让工具走向开源的可行路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://support.mozilla.org/en-US/kb/block-ads-firefox-ios" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Firefox for iOS now has a native adblocker</span><span class='zh'>iOS 版 Firefox 现已内置原生广告拦截器</span></a><span class="dom">support.mozilla.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Firefox iOS now ships with a native ad-blocker, improving privacy and page speed by blocking trackers and ads at the browser level.</span><span class='zh'>iOS 版 Firefox 增添原生广告拦截功能，通过浏览器层面屏蔽跟踪与广告来提升隐私和页面加载速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Firefox iOS now ships with a native ad-blocker, improving privacy and page speed by blocking trackers and ads at the browser level. This reduces data usage and helps users bypass some invasive tracking without relying on external extensions.</span><span class='zh'>iOS 版 Firefox 增添原生广告拦截功能，通过浏览器层面屏蔽跟踪与广告来提升隐私和页面加载速度。减少数据使用，并在无需外部扩展的情况下改善用户体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/xoreaxeaxeax/skitter-creek-bath-salts" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spaghettifying DRAM</span><span class='zh'>将 DRAM 拆解为意面状结构</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spaghettifying DRAM examines how unusual or chaotic memory layouts can affect latency, bandwidth, and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>Spaghettifying DRAM 探讨了非传统、混乱的内存布局如何影响延迟、带宽与可靠性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spaghettifying DRAM examines how unusual or chaotic memory layouts can affect latency, bandwidth, and reliability. The discussion highlights the ongoing tension between memory hardware complexity and software efficiency, suggesting approaches for hardware-aware optimization.</span><span class='zh'>Spaghettifying DRAM 探讨了非传统、混乱的内存布局如何影响延迟、带宽与可靠性。该讨论凸显了内存硬件复杂性与软件效率之间的矛盾，并提出面向硬件的优化思路。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Examines why some users perceive apps as delivering a superior experience than web equivalents.</span><span class='zh'>探讨为何有些用户认为桌面/移动应用在体验上优于网页版。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An eclectic reading list contrasts crime-focused topics with botany, using the tension between risk and growth to illustrate how different domains reward careful inquiry and robust systems thinking.</span><span class='zh'>这是一份以犯罪题材与植物学之间的对比为主的阅读清单，揭示不同领域对好奇心、方法论和系统思维的奖励机制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An eclectic reading list contrasts crime-focused topics with botany, using the tension between risk and growth to illustrate how different domains reward careful inquiry and robust systems thinking. It offers cross-disciplinary insights into detection, growth, and learning strategies valuable to technologists.</span><span class='zh'>这是一份以犯罪题材与植物学之间的对比为主的阅读清单，揭示不同领域对好奇心、方法论和系统思维的奖励机制。通过跨学科的对照，帮助科技工作者理解数据模式、风险评估与学习策略。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A blogger reveals that misconfigured telecom routing accidentally logged hundreds of thousands of calls destined for military bases.</span><span class='zh'>博主披露，由于配置或路由错误，数十万通电话被错误路由记录指向军事基地。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A blogger reveals that misconfigured telecom routing accidentally logged hundreds of thousands of calls destined for military bases. The incident highlights telecom privacy risks and the potential for misrouting to expose sensitive endpoints. It raises questions about how edge-case routing bugs are monitored and mitigated.</span><span class='zh'>博主披露，由于配置或路由错误，数十万通电话被错误路由记录指向军事基地。此事凸显电信隐私风险与错误路由对敏感终端的潜在影响，也引发对边缘路由漏洞的监控与缓解的关注。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.docker.com/products/docker-sandboxes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.docker.com/app/uploads/2024/02/docker-default-meta-image-1110x583.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='docker.com' data-c='#14b8a6' data-tag='DevOps'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">103</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.docker.com/products/docker-sandboxes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Docker Sandboxes – Disposable, isolated sandboxes for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>Docker 沙盒：面向 AI 代理的一次性隔离沙箱</span></a><span class="dom">docker.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Docker Sandboxes provide disposable, isolated environments for running AI agents, enabling safer experimentation and reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>Docker Sandboxes 提供一次性、隔离的运行环境，专为 AI 代理设计，降低实验风险并提高可重复性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Docker Sandboxes provide disposable, isolated environments for running AI agents, enabling safer experimentation and reproducibility. They offer controlled dependencies and snapshot-like configurations to prevent cross-agent interference. This helps teams prototype AI tasks without risking the host system.</span><span class='zh'>Docker Sandboxes 提供一次性、隔离的运行环境，专为 AI 代理设计，降低实验风险并提高可重复性。通过可控依赖和快照式配置，开发者可以在不影响宿主系统的情况下安全试验复杂的 AI 任务。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='DevOps' style='--c:#14b8a6'>DevOps</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>663</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49239751' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>368</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-49182996" data-id="49182996" data-tags="Open Source|DevOps" data-score="658" data-comments="324" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49182996" data-time="1785938333">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-os/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cloudflare OS: an open platform for agents, apps, and work</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare OS：面向代理、应用与工作的新开放平台</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cloudflare unveils Cloudflare OS, an open platform designed to host agents, apps, and work workflows at the edge.</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 推出 Cloudflare OS，这是一个面向在边缘运行的代理、应用和工作流的开放平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cloudflare unveils Cloudflare OS, an open platform designed to host agents, apps, and work workflows at the edge. It aims to broaden extensibility across Cloudflare&#x27;s edge network by letting developers deploy自治代理和应用并与现有服务集成，提升边缘计算的可扩展性与安全性。</span><span class='zh'>Cloudflare 推出 Cloudflare OS，这是一个面向在边缘运行的代理、应用和工作流的开放平台。通过允许开发者部署自治代理和应用并与现有服务集成，提升边缘计算的可扩展性与安全性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively.</span><span class='zh'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>qm is an open-source harness that coordinates multiple agents to work on tasks collaboratively. It provides orchestration, tasking, and visibility across agent-driven workflows, helping teams tackle complex problems more efficiently.</span><span class='zh'>qm 是一个开源的多人代理协调框架，支持在工作场景中让多个代理协同完成任务。它提供编排、任务分发和对代理驱动工作流的可视化，帮助团队更高效地解决复杂问题。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.effort.news/uk-lobby" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.effort.news/images/article-graphics/uk-lobby/reset-bipartite-graph-cover.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='effort.news' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">108</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.effort.news/uk-lobby" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The UK&#x27;s war on anonymity has come to America</span><span class='zh'>英国对匿名性的打压蔓延至美国</span></a><span class="dom">effort.news</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A UK-style push to curb online anonymity is gaining traction in the US, with lawmakers favoring identity verification and traceability.</span><span class='zh'>英国对匿名性的打压正在美国获得关注，立法者日益支持身份核验和可追溯性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A UK-style push to curb online anonymity is gaining traction in the US, with lawmakers favoring identity verification and traceability. The move signals a broader shift toward user accountability that could reshape platform moderation, data sharing, and civil liberties. For technologists, the takeaway is that privacy-centered design may need stronger defaults and clearer opt-outs.</span><span class='zh'>英国对匿名性的打压正在美国获得关注，立法者日益支持身份核验和可追溯性。此举预示着向用户问责的全球性潮流，可能影响平台治理、数据共享和公民自由。对技术人来说，隐私友好设计需要更强的默认保护与清晰的选择权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ngrok.com/blog/compression-is-prediction" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Compression is prediction</span><span class='zh'>压缩即预测</span></a><span class="dom">ngrok.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Data compression is fundamentally about predicting the next symbol; improving a compressor equates to a better predictor.</span><span class='zh'>数据压缩本质上是对下一个符号的预测；提升压缩效果等同于改进预测能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Data compression is fundamentally about predicting the next symbol; improving a compressor equates to a better predictor.</span><span class='zh'>数据压缩本质上是对下一个符号的预测；提升压缩效果等同于改进预测能力。这一观点对机器学习、数据存储与推理具有实际启示，适用于希望把压缩思路融入模型设计的开发者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bandarlabs.github.io/Cobalt/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kobo can run apps now</span><span class='zh'>Kobo 电子书阅读器现在可以运行应用程序</span></a><span class="dom">bandarlabs.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kobo devices now support running apps through a new runtime, expanding their functionality beyond ebooks.</span><span class='zh'>Kobo 电子书阅读器现在可以在设备上运行应用程序，扩展了其功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kobo devices now support running apps through a new runtime, expanding their functionality beyond ebooks. This brings on-device tools and services closer to rival ecosystems, potentially changing how users interact with e-readers. For developers, it opens a new platform with modest hardware requirements.</span><span class='zh'>Kobo 电子书阅读器现在可以在设备上运行应用程序，扩展了其功能。此举将对设备生态带来新的工具与服务，改变用户使用电子书阅读器的方式；对开发者而言，意味着在相对低配硬件上进入新平台的机会。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">111</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tailscale/tailcat" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tailcat – Like netcat, but over Tailscale’s data plane</span><span class='zh'>Tailcat：在 Tailscale 数据平面上的 netcat 替代品</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tailcat provides a netcat-style data channel that rides on Tailscale&#x27;s data plane, allowing direct, authenticated connections between devices regardless of NATs.</span><span class='zh'>Tailcat 在 Tailscale 的数据平面上实现类似 netcat 的数据通道，能够在 NAT 穿透的设备之间建立经过认证的直接连接。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tailcat provides a netcat-style data channel that rides on Tailscale&#x27;s data plane, allowing direct, authenticated connections between devices regardless of NATs. It offers a lightweight way to test services, tunnel traffic, and diagnose network issues without exposing endpoints publicly.</span><span class='zh'>Tailcat 在 Tailscale 的数据平面上实现类似 netcat 的数据通道，能够在 NAT 穿透的设备之间建立经过认证的直接连接。它为测试、隧道传输和网络排错提供了一个轻量的替代方案，无需暴露公网端点。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Memory prices surged 500% year over year, with high-end DDR5 kits reaching thousands of dollars per kit; supply-chain pressures and demand for AI workloads drive the spike.</span><span class='zh'>内存价格在一年内暴涨约 500%，高端 DDR5 套件价格甚至达到数千美元。</span></p>
